  <section class="services" id="services">
    <h2 class="big fade" data-t="serv" style="transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0s;">Services</h2>
    <div class="slist" id="slist"><div class="sitem fade" data-d="0" style="transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0s;"><div class="snum">01</div><div><h3 class="sname">Websites and landing pages</h3><p class="sdesc">A first screen that holds attention: video, 3D, animation on scroll and cursor. Phone version, English and Russian, leads to your Telegram.</p></div></div><div class="sitem fade" data-d="0.1" style="transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0.1s;"><div class="snum">02</div><div><h3 class="sname">Web services and dashboards</h3><p class="sdesc">Admin panels, client portals, catalogs, calculators, booking: front end, back end, database and deployment, all in one pair of hands.</p></div></div><div class="sitem fade" data-d="0.2" style="transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0.2s;"><div class="snum">03</div><div><h3 class="sname">AI agents and chatbots</h3><p class="sdesc">They answer customers from your price list and stock, take orders and hand the tricky cases to you. My own product is an AI seller for Avito chats.</p></div></div><div class="sitem fade" data-d="0.30000000000000004" style="transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0.3s;"><div class="snum">04</div><div><h3 class="sname">Telegram bots and mini apps</h3><p class="sdesc">Bookings, orders, forms, broadcasts and full mini apps inside Telegram. Leads land in your chat or CRM.</p></div></div><div class="sitem fade" data-d="0.4" style="transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0.4s;"><div class="snum">05</div><div><h3 class="sname">Integrations and automation</h3><p class="sdesc">I connect the site, the bot, amoCRM, Bitrix24, 1C, spreadsheets and any API. Parsers, scripts and reports take the manual routine off your team.</p></div></div><div class="sitem fade" data-d="0.5" style="transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0.5s;"><div class="snum">06</div><div><h3 class="sname">3D and WebGL</h3><p class="sdesc">A configurator where the buyer builds the product, a 3D showcase, interactive shader scenes.</p></div></div><div class="sitem fade" data-d="0.6000000000000001" style="transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0.6s;"><div class="snum">07</div><div><h3 class="sname">Mobile and desktop apps</h3><p class="sdesc">iOS and Android on React Native, desktop apps, browser extensions: one codebase, released to the stores.</p></div></div><div class="sitem fade" data-d="0.7000000000000001" style="transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0.7s;"><div class="snum">08</div><div><h3 class="sname">Back end, DevOps and support</h3><p class="sdesc">APIs on Go, Python and Node, databases, queues, Docker, CI/CD and monitoring. I take over existing projects: I read someone else’s code, fix it and make it faster.</p></div></div></div>
    <p class="snote fade" data-t="snote" style="transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0s;">A website — from $290, 5–7 days. 50% upfront, the rest on acceptance.</p>
    <p class="snote fade" id="oplata" data-t="oplata" style="margin-top: 14px; transform: translate(0px, 30px); transition-duration: 0.7s; transition-delay: 0s;">How to pay: bank transfer in USD (SWIFT), USDT or USDC. Small fixes are paid after you check them. Bank and wallet details are in the invoice.</p>
  </section>

  { one: 'hot-ice', cat: EN ? '3D and WebGL · GPU simulation' : '3D и WebGL · симуляция на GPU', btn: EN ? 'Open' : 'Открыть' },
  { one: 'apex-pulse', cat: EN ? 'Website · scroll-driven video' : 'Сайт · видео на скролле', btn: EN ? 'Open' : 'Открыть' },
  { one: 'orb', cat: EN ? '3D and WebGL · audio-reactive' : '3D и WebGL · реагирует на звук', btn: EN ? 'Open' : 'Открыть' },
  { one: 'xportfolio-hero', cat: EN ? 'Website · portfolio' : 'Сайт · портфолио', btn: EN ? 'Open' : 'Открыть' },
  { one: 'golden-portal', cat: EN ? 'Website · art gallery' : 'Сайт · арт-галерея', btn: EN ? 'Open' : 'Открыть' }
].filter(p => p.one ? bySlug[p.one] : p.works.every(s => bySlug[s]));
$('plist').innerHTML = PROJ.map((p, i) => {
  const w = (p.works || [p.one]).map(s => bySlug[s]);
  return `<div class="pwrap"><div class="pcard" style="top:${i * 28}px"><div class="ptop"><div class="pnum">0${i + 1}</div>` +
    `<div class="pmeta"><span class="pcat">${p.one ? p.cat : (EN ? 'Section' : 'Раздел')}</span><h3 class="pname">${p.one ? esc(tx(w[0])[0]) : CAT[p.go]}</h3></div>` +
    (p.one ? `<a class="live" ${attrs(w[0])}>${p.btn}</a></div><div class="pimgs one">${tile(hq(w[0]), '')}</div>`
      : `<a class="live" href="#all" data-go="${p.go}">${EN ? 'Whole section' : 'Весь раздел'}</a></div>` +
        `<div class="pimgs"><div class="pc1">${tile(w[0], 'i1')}${tile(w[1], 'i2')}</div><div class="pc2">${tile(w[2], '')}</div></div>`) +
    `</div></div>`;
}).join('');

// ленты после портрета: только сайты (раздел «Сайты»), самые красивые превью; новые MotionSites вперемешку со старыми (24.09); каждая тройкой
const LENTA = ['aurum', 'big-bang-origin', 'golden-portal', 'fox-404', 'jungle-train', 'slowlight', 'glitch-pulse', 'private-jets',
  'apex-pulse', 'dreams', 'orbit-engineers', 'space-planet', 'xportfolio-hero', 'sky-estate', 'luxury-hero', 'cinematic-brand',
  'crystal-form', 'motionz-premium-landing-page', 'cyberpunk-reveal', 'cursor-follow', 'asme', 'nova-space-systems', 'figurines', 'investment-hero',
  'synth-mode', 'intelligent-operations', 'orbit-flora', 'cinematic-404', 'cosmic', 'creative-agency', 'bio-active', 'yoga-coach',
  'rybtseh-pokaz', 'evr-ventures', 'audio-showcase', 'space-voyages', 'data-shield', 'framelix-3d-studios', 'jewelry-store', 'innovation-lab',
  'sunvalley-pokaz', 'orbis-nft', 'archive', 'lab', 'vertex-sci', 'new-era-automotive-hero']
  .map(s => bySlug[s]).filter(Boolean), half = Math.ceil(LENTA.length / 2);
const rows = [[$('r1'), LENTA.slice(0, half), 1], [$('r2'), LENTA.slice(half), -1]];
rows.forEach(([row, list]) => { row.innerHTML = [...list, ...list, ...list].map(w => tile(w, '', `<span>${esc(tx(w)[0])}</span>`)).join(''); });

// стена: вкладки по разделам; во «Всех» первые 12, остальные по кнопке
const FIRST = 12, grid = $('grid');
let razvernuto = false;
grid.innerHTML = WORKS.map((w, i) => `<a class="card${i >= FIRST ? ' more' : ''}" data-cat="${w.cat}" ${attrs(w)}>` +
  `<div class="media" style="background-image:url(${poster(w)})">${vid(w)}</div><div class="cbody"><span class="tag">${CAT[w.cat]}</span>` +
  `<h4>${esc(tx(w)[0])}</h4><p>${esc(tx(w)[1])}</p><span class="open">${w.lb ? (EN ? 'Watch' : 'Смотреть') : (EN ? 'Open' : 'Открыть')}${OUT}</span></div></a>`).join('');
$('wallh').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<span class="cnt">' + WORKS.length + '</span>');
$('tabs').innerHTML = [['all', EN ? 'All' : 'Все', WORKS.length], ...Object.keys(CAT).map(c => [c, CAT[c], WORKS.filter(w => w.cat === c).length])]
  .map(([c, t, k]) => `<button class="tab${c === 'all' ? ' on' : ''}" type="button" data-f="${c}">${t}<i>${k}</i></button>`).join('');
grid.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', `<div class="allwrap" id="allwrap"><button class="live" id="allbtn" type="button">${(EN ? 'Show all ' : 'Показать все ') + WORKS.length}</button></div>`);
function filtr(c) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.f === c));
  grid.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(k => k.classList.toggle('off', c !== 'all' && k.dataset.cat !== c));
  grid.classList.toggle('all', c !== 'all' || razvernuto);
  $('allwrap').hidden = c !== 'all' || razvernuto;
}
$('tabs').addEventListener('click', e => { const b = e.target.closest('.tab'); if (b) filtr(b.dataset.f); });
$('allbtn').addEventListener('click', () => { razvernuto = true; filtr('all'); });
document.querySelectorAll('[data-go]').forEach(a => a.addEventListener('click', () => filtr(a.dataset.go)));

// окно для работ без своей страницы: ролик, картинки, звук
document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<div class="lb" id="lb" hidden><button class="lbx" id="lbx" type="button" aria-label="${EN ? 'Close' : 'Закрыть'}">×</button><div class="lbin" id="lbin"></div></div>`);
const lb = $('lb'), lbin = $('lbin');
function otkryt(w) {
  const x = w.lb;
  let h = `<h3>${esc(tx(w)[0])}</h3><p>${esc(tx(w)[1])}</p>`;
  if (x.video) h += `<video src="${x.video}" controls autoplay loop playsinline></video>`;
  (x.imgs || []).forEach(src => { h += `<img src="${src}" alt="">`; });
  if (x.audio) h += x.audio.map(a => `<div class="au"><span>${EN ? a[1] : a[0]}</span><audio src="${a[2]}" controls preload="none"></audio></div>`).join('');
  if (x.href) h += `<a class="cbtn" href="${url(x)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${EN ? 'Open the site' : 'Открыть сайт'}</a>`;
  lbin.innerHTML = h; lb.hidden = false; document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
}
// работа со своей страницей: живой сайт в окне — можно листать самому; Ctrl/колёсико — как раньше, во вкладку
function stranica(w, u) {
  lbin.innerHTML = `<div class="lbhd"><h3>${esc(tx(w)[0])}</h3><a class="live" href="${u}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${EN ? 'Open in a new tab' : 'Открыть в новой вкладке'}</a></div>` +
    `<iframe src="${u}" title="${esc(tx(w)[0])}" allow="autoplay; fullscreen"></iframe>`;
  lbin.classList.add('pg'); lb.hidden = false; document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
}
function zakryt() { lbin.innerHTML = ''; lbin.classList.remove('pg'); lb.hidden = true; document.body.style.overflow = ''; }
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const a = e.target.closest('[data-lb]'); if (a) { e.preventDefault(); otkryt(bySlug[a.dataset.lb]); return; }
  const p = e.target.closest('[data-pg]');
  if (p && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.shiftKey && e.button === 0) { e.preventDefault(); stranica(bySlug[p.dataset.pg], p.getAttribute('href')); }
});
$('lbx').addEventListener('click', zakryt);
lb.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === lb) zakryt(); });
addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape' && !lb.hidden) zakryt(); });

// превью играют, только пока видны
const vio = new IntersectionObserver(es => es.forEach(e => {
  const v = e.target;
  if (e.isIntersecting) { if (!v.src) v.src = v.dataset.src; v.play().catch(() => {}); } else if (v.src) v.pause();
}), { rootMargin: '150px' });
document.querySelectorAll('video[data-src]').forEach(v => vio.observe(v));

// появление
const io = new IntersectionObserver(es => es.forEach(e => { if (e.isIntersecting) { e.target.classList.add('in'); io.unobserve(e.target); } }), { rootMargin: '50px' });
document.querySelectorAll('.fade').forEach(el => {
  const d = el.dataset;
  el.style.transform = `translate(${d.x || 0}px,${d.y !== undefined ? d.y : 30}px)`;
  el.style.transitionDuration = (d.dur || 0.7) + 's';
  el.style.transitionDelay = (d.d || 0) + 's';
  io.observe(el);
});

// заголовок в одну строку: ужать, если не влез
const hh = $('hh');
function fit() {
  hh.style.fontSize = '';
  const w = hh.parentElement.clientWidth, sw = hh.scrollWidth;
  if (sw > w) hh.style.fontSize = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(hh).fontSize) * w / sw * 0.97 + 'px';
}
fit();
document.fonts && document.fonts.ready.then(fit);
addEventListener('resize', fit);

// «Обо мне» по буквам
const anim = $('anim');
anim.innerHTML = anim.textContent.split(' ').map(w => '<span class="w">' + [...w].map(c => '<span class="c">' + c + '</span>').join('') + '</span>').join(' ');
const chars = [...anim.querySelectorAll('.c')];

let rowW = [0, 0];
const measure = () => { rowW = rows.map(([row]) => (row.scrollWidth + 12) / 3); };
measure();
addEventListener('resize', measure);

let lastY = null;
function frame(now) {
  const y = scrollY, vh = innerHeight;
  if (y !== lastY) {
    lastY = y;
    // ленты едут от прокрутки; база — минус одна тройка, чтобы слева не было пустоты
    const top = $('marquee').getBoundingClientRect().top + y;
    const off = (y - top + vh) * 0.3;
    rows.forEach(([row, , dir], i) => { row.style.transform = `translateX(${dir * (off - 200) - rowW[i]}px)`; });
    // буквы проявляются от прокрутки
    const r = anim.getBoundingClientRect();
    const p = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (vh * 0.8 - r.top) / (r.height + vh * 0.6)));
    const n = chars.length;
    chars.forEach((c, i) => { const o = 0.2 + 0.8 * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (p - i / n) * n)); if (c._o !== o) { c._o = o; c.style.opacity = o; } });
    // карточки проектов складываются стопкой
    const pl = $('plist'), pr = pl.getBoundingClientRect();
    const pp = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, -pr.top / Math.max(1, pr.height - vh)));
    pl.querySelectorAll('.pcard').forEach((c, i, all) => {
      const target = 1 - (all.length - 1 - i) * 0.03, from = i / all.length;
      const k = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (pp - from) / (1 - from)));
      c.style.transform = `scale(${1 - (1 - target) * k})`;
    });
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
})();
</script><div class="lb" id="lb" hidden=""><button class="lbx" id="lbx" type="button" aria-label="Close">×</button><div class="lbin" id="lbin"></div></div>
<script type="module">
// первый экран: лицо Артура, под курсором проступает шлем Короля-лича (js/golova.js из portfolio-new)
import { golova } from './js/golova.js';
const holst = document.getElementById('golova');
if (document.createElement('canvas').getContext('webgl2')) try { window.gadWait = golova(holst); } catch (e) { console.warn('портрет не поднялся:', e); holst.hidden = true; }
else { holst.hidden = true; document.querySelector('.hint').hidden = true; }
</script>
